Title: The memorable and tragical history of the persecution in Africke, under Genserick and
    Hunnerick, Arrian kings of the Vandals, with the life of S. Fulgentius
Creator: Victor of Vita (fl. c. 484) (English translation of 1605, translator unnamed)
Publisher: England, printed with licence by the English secret press, 1605
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: 1605 publication, out of US copyright by date; the item carries no copyright field of
    its own, so the imprint date is the basis, read directly 2026-10-23.
Source: Internet Archive item bim_early-english-books-1475-1640_the-memorable-and-tragic_victor-bp-of-vita_1605 (archive.org/details/bim_early-english-books-1475-1640_the-memorable-and-tragic_victor-bp-of-vita_1605), fetched directly
    (curl, raw bytes) from the item's own _djvu.txt full-text file, 2026-10-23, Library
    thread (Era III).
Provenance note: Raw, uncorrected djvu OCR (rough (microfilm scan, long s)). Runs of 2+ spaces
    collapsed to one; no wording changed. Sections: the History by book and chapter; the
    Life of Fulgentius follows. Credit line for a reader-facing page: "The memorable and
    tragical history of the persecution in Africke (England, 1605)". Whole text
    republishable.
Content note: The only English text of Victor of Vita that is public domain, in 17th-century English
    by a Recusant translator; use with the Latin editions.
